  <script>
    const payload = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('predictionData').textContent);
    const examples = payload.examples;
    const state = { status: 'all', room: 'all', k: 'all', violation: 'all' };
    const palette = ['#9fc4a1','#dfb36b','#92b9d5','#c2a8d7','#d89f99','#a7c7c2','#c7bd8c','#aeb8d4'];

    function esc(value) {
      return String(value).replace(/[&<>'"]/g, ch => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[ch]));
    }
    function hashColor(value) {
      let hash = 0;
      for (const ch of value) hash = ((hash << 5) - hash + ch.charCodeAt(0)) | 0;
      return palette[Math.abs(hash) % palette.length];
    }
    function fmt(value, digits = 2) { return Number(value).toFixed(digits); }
    function actionClass(value) { return `action-${value.toLowerCase()}`; }
    function actionLabel(value) {
      return ({KEEP:'KEEP',TRANSLATE:'平移',ROTATE:'旋转',BOTH:'平移+旋转'})[value] || value;
    }
    function violationText(value) {
      const map = { boundary: '越界', collision: '碰撞', opening: 'Opening', clean: '无违规' };
      return value.map(v => map[v]).join(' + ');
    }
    function violationCountText(value) {
      return `边界 ${value.boundary} · 碰撞 ${value.collision} · Opening ${value.opening}`;
    }
    function deltaText(delta) {
      return `[${fmt(delta[0])}, ${fmt(delta[1])}, ${fmt(delta[2])}]`;
    }

    function makeSvg(example, poses, mode) {
      const width = 520, height = 382, padding = 28;
      const room = example.room;
      const scale = Math.min((width - padding * 2) / room.length, (height - padding * 2) / room.width);
      const roomW = room.length * scale, roomH = room.width * scale;
      const ox = (width - roomW) / 2, oy = (height - roomH) / 2;
      const sx = x => ox + (x + room.length / 2) * scale;
      const sy = y => oy + (room.width / 2 - y) * scale;
      const parts = [
        `<svg class="scene-svg" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="${esc(mode)} scene">`,
        `<rect x="${fmt(ox)}" y="${fmt(oy)}" width="${fmt(roomW)}" height="${fmt(roomH)}" fill="#fff" stroke="#27323c" stroke-width="3"/>`
      ];
      for (const opening of example.openings) {
        const color = opening.kind === 'door' ? '#d74841' : '#348bca';
        parts.push(`<rect x="${fmt(sx(opening.x) - opening.width * scale / 2)}" y="${fmt(sy(opening.y) - opening.depth * scale / 2)}" width="${fmt(opening.width * scale)}" height="${fmt(opening.depth * scale)}" fill="${color}" fill-opacity="0.18" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
      }
      if (mode !== 'input') {
        example.current_poses.forEach((pose, index) => {
          const item = example.furniture[index];
          if (item.predicted_action === 'KEEP' && mode === 'prediction') return;
          if (item.target_action === 'KEEP' && mode === 'target') return;
          parts.push(`<rect x="${fmt(-pose.width * scale / 2)}" y="${fmt(-pose.depth * scale / 2)}" width="${fmt(pose.width * scale)}" height="${fmt(pose.depth * scale)}" transform="translate(${fmt(sx(pose.x))} ${fmt(sy(pose.y))}) rotate(${fmt(-pose.yaw * 180 / Math.PI)})" fill="none" stroke="#74808b" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/>`);
        });
      }
      poses.forEach((pose, index) => {
        const item = example.furniture[index];
        const action = mode === 'prediction' ? item.predicted_action : item.target_action;
        const changed = action !== 'KEEP';
        const fill = changed ? '#e9b55d' : hashColor(item.category);
        const stroke = item.action_correct || mode !== 'prediction' ? '#42515e' : '#c43c35';
        const cx = sx(pose.x), cy = sy(pose.y);
        parts.push(`<g transform="translate(${fmt(cx)} ${fmt(cy)}) rotate(${fmt(-pose.yaw * 180 / Math.PI)})">`);
        parts.push(`<rect x="${fmt(-pose.width * scale / 2)}" y="${fmt(-pose.depth * scale / 2)}" width="${fmt(pose.width * scale)}" height="${fmt(pose.depth * scale)}" rx="2" fill="${fill}" fill-opacity="0.82" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="${item.action_correct ? 1.4 : 2.4}"/>`);
        parts.push(`</g>`);
        const arrowLength = Math.max(10, Math.min(26, pose.depth * scale * 0.44));
        const ax = cx + Math.sin(pose.yaw) * arrowLength;
        const ay = cy + Math.cos(pose.yaw) * arrowLength;
        parts.push(`<line x1="${fmt(cx)}" y1="${fmt(cy)}" x2="${fmt(ax)}" y2="${fmt(ay)}" stroke="#18212b" stroke-width="2"/>`);
        parts.push(`<circle cx="${fmt(ax)}" cy="${fmt(ay)}" r="2.6" fill="#18212b"/>`);
        const short = item.category.replace('_table',' tbl').replace('console_','').slice(0, 12);
        parts.push(`<text x="${fmt(cx)}" y="${fmt(cy + 4)}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="600" fill="#17202a" style="paint-order:stroke;stroke:#fff;stroke-width:2px;stroke-linejoin:round">${esc(short)}</text>`);
      });
      parts.push('</svg>');
      return parts.join('');
    }

    function actionRows(example) {
      return example.furniture.map(item => {
        const rowClass = item.action_correct ? '' : 'mismatch';
        return `<tr class="${rowClass}">
          <td>${esc(item.id)}</td>
          <td>${esc(item.category)}</td>
          <td class="${actionClass(item.target_action)}">${actionLabel(item.target_action)}</td>
          <td class="${actionClass(item.predicted_action)}">${actionLabel(item.predicted_action)}</td>
          <td>${deltaText(item.target_delta)}</td>
          <td>${deltaText(item.predicted_delta)}</td>
          <td>${item.action_correct ? '匹配' : '不匹配'}</td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    function exampleHtml(example, index) {
      const statusClass = example.action_all_correct ? 'success' : 'review';
      const statusText = example.action_all_correct ? '动作全匹配' : '需复核';
      return `<article class="example" data-status="${example.action_all_correct ? 'success' : 'review'}" data-room="${esc(example.room.type)}" data-k="${esc(example.k_group)}" data-violations="${esc(example.violation_tags.join(','))}">
        <div class="example-header">
          <div class="example-title">
            <h2>${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} · ${esc(example.scene_id)}</h2>
            <span class="badge ${statusClass}">${statusText}</span>
            <span class="badge neutral">${esc(example.room.type)}</span>
            <span class="badge neutral">K=${example.k}</span>
            <span class="badge neutral">${violationText(example.violation_tags)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="header-facts">
            <span>Furniture <strong>${example.furniture.length}</strong></span>
            <span>违规 <strong>${example.violations_before.total} → ${example.violations_after.total}</strong></span>
            <span>Seed <strong>${example.seed}</strong></span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="scene-grid">
          <section class="scene-panel">
            <div class="panel-heading"><span>受扰动输入</span><span>${violationCountText(example.violations_before)}</span></div>
            ${makeSvg(example, example.current_poses, 'input')}
          </section>
          <section class="scene-panel">
            <div class="panel-heading"><span>模型预测修复</span><span>${violationCountText(example.violations_after)}</span></div>
            ${makeSvg(example, example.predicted_poses, 'prediction')}
          </section>
          <section class="scene-panel">
            <div class="panel-heading"><span>规划目标</span><span>${violationCountText(example.violations_target)}</span></div>
            ${makeSvg(example, example.target_poses, 'target')}
          </section>
        </div>
        <details class="details">
          <summary>对象动作与 delta 对照</summary>
          <div class="action-table-wrap">
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Object</th><th>Category</th><th>目标 action</th><th>预测 action</th><th>目标 [dx,dy,dyaw]</th><th>预测 [dx,dy,dyaw]</th><th>结果</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>${actionRows(example)}</tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </details>
      </article>`;
    }

    function filterGroup(label, key, values) {
      return `<div class="filter-group"><span class="filter-label">${label}</span>${values.map(([value, text]) => `<button class="filter-button ${state[key] === value ? 'active' : ''}" type="button" data-filter-key="${key}" data-filter-value="${value}">${text}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    }
    function renderFilters() {
      document.getElementById('filters').innerHTML = [
        filterGroup('状态', 'status', [['all','全部'],['success','全匹配'],['review','需复核']]),
        filterGroup('房型', 'room', [['all','全部'],['bedroom','卧室'],['living_room','客厅'],['dining_room','餐厅'],['home_office','办公室']]),
        filterGroup('K', 'k', [['all','全部'],['0','0'],['1','1'],['2-3','2-3'],['4-5','4-5'],['6+','6+']]),
        filterGroup('违规', 'violation', [['all','全部'],['boundary','越界'],['collision','碰撞'],['opening','Opening'],['clean','无违规']])
      ].join('');
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-button').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        state[button.dataset.filterKey] = button.dataset.filterValue;
        renderFilters();
        renderExamples();
      }));
    }
    function visibleExamples() {
      return examples.filter(example => {
        if (state.status !== 'all' && (example.action_all_correct ? 'success' : 'review') !== state.status) return false;
        if (state.room !== 'all' && example.room.type !== state.room) return false;
        if (state.k !== 'all' && example.k_group !== state.k) return false;
        if (state.violation !== 'all' && !example.violation_tags.includes(state.violation)) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }
    function renderExamples() {
      const visible = visibleExamples();
      document.getElementById('resultsLine').textContent = `显示 ${visible.length} / ${examples.length} 个案例`;
      document.getElementById('examples').innerHTML = visible.length
        ? visible.map((example) => exampleHtml(example, examples.indexOf(example))).join('')
        : '<div class="empty">当前筛选条件下没有案例</div>';
    }
    function renderSummary() {
      const successes = examples.filter(v => v.action_all_correct).length;
      const cleared = examples.filter(v => v.geometry_cleared).length;
      const highK = examples.filter(v => v.k >= 6).length;
      const before = examples.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v.violations_before.total, 0);
      const after = examples.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v.violations_after.total, 0);
      document.getElementById('summary').innerHTML = `
        <div class="metric"><strong>${examples.length}</strong><span>展示案例</span></div>
        <div class="metric good"><strong>${successes}</strong><span>动作全匹配</span></div>
        <div class="metric good"><strong>${cleared}</strong><span>预测后硬违规清零</span></div>
        <div class="metric"><strong>${highK}</strong><span>K ≥ 6 案例</span></div>
        <div class="metric warn"><strong>${before} → ${after}</strong><span>展示集硬违规总数</span></div>`;
      document.getElementById('runMeta').textContent = `observable_v5 · checkpoint epoch ${payload.checkpoint_extra.epoch} · test seed ${payload.test_seed}`;
    }
    renderSummary();
    renderFilters();
    renderExamples();
    document.body.dataset.layoutDebug = ['innerWidth', '.example', '.scene-grid', '.scene-panel', '.scene-svg', '.panel-heading'].map((selector) => {
      if (selector === 'innerWidth') return `innerWidth:${window.innerWidth}`;
      const element = document.querySelector(selector);
      return `${selector}:${element?.getBoundingClientRect().width}/${element?.scrollWidth}`;
    }).join('|');
  </script>


</body></html>
